		   NULL, "Beni o bakışlarının esaretinden kurtarıp sessizce iki üç adım geri çekildi.", Line_Normal);
	ClearMessage();

	PlaySE( 4, "wa_003", 56, 64 );
//　その途端、俺のひざがかくんと抜け、だらしなく両ひざを地面につける＠
	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode)) { OutputLineAll("", NULL, Line_ContinueAfterTyping); }
	OutputLine(NULL, "　その途端、俺のひざがかくんと抜け、だらしなく両ひざを地面につける。",
		   NULL, "Tam o anda dizlerimin bağı çözüldü ve iki dizimin üzerine acınası bir halde çöktüm.", GetGlobalFlag(GLinemodeSp));
	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode)) { ClearMessage(); } else { OutputLineAll(NULL, "\n\n", Line_ContinueAfterTyping); }


//　……感情のない笑みを浮かべたレナと、その前に呆然とひざまずく俺……＠
	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode)) { OutputLineAll("", NULL, Line_ContinueAfterTyping); }
	OutputLine(NULL, "　……感情のない笑みを浮かべたレナと、その前に呆然とひざまずく俺……。",
		   NULL, "...Yüzünde duygusuz bir gülümseme taşıyan Rena ve onun önünde sersemlemiş bir halde diz çöken ben...", GetGlobalFlag(GLinemodeSp));
	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode)) { ClearMessage(); } else { OutputLineAll(NULL, "\n", Line_ContinueAfterTyping); }



//　それはきっと異様な光景だったに違いない…￥
	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode)) { OutputLineAll("", NULL, Line_ContinueAfterTyping); }
	OutputLine(NULL, "　それはきっと異様な光景だったに違いない…。",
		   NULL, "Dışarıdan bakınca kesinlikle çok tuhaf bir manzara olmalıydı...", Line_Normal);
	ClearMessage();

//　レナはそんな無様な俺を、あざ笑いもしなければ手を差し伸べもしなかった＠
	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode)) { OutputLineAll("", NULL, Line_ContinueAfterTyping); }
	OutputLine(NULL, "　レナはそんな無様な俺を、あざ笑いもしなければ手を差し伸べもしなかった。",
		   NULL, "Rena benim bu acınası halime ne alaycı bir şekilde güldü ne de elini uzattı.", GetGlobalFlag(GLinemodeSp));
	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode)) { ClearMessage(); } else { OutputLineAll(NULL, "\n", Line_ContinueAfterTyping); }



//　だが、俺の眼をただ静かにじっと射抜き、立つことも逃げることも許さない＠
	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode)) { OutputLineAll("", NULL, Line_ContinueAfterTyping); }
	OutputLine(NULL, "　だが、俺の眼をただ静かにじっと射抜き、立つことも逃げることも許さない。",
		   NULL, "Ama gözlerimin içine sessizce ve dimdik bakmaya devam ederek ne ayağa kalkmama ne de kaçmama izin verdi.", GetGlobalFlag(GLinemodeSp));
	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode)) { ClearMessage(); } else { OutputLineAll(NULL, "\n\n", Line_ContinueAfterTyping); }


//　金属バットは確かに俺の手にあった＠
	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode)) { OutputLineAll("", NULL, Line_ContinueAfterTyping); }
	OutputLine(NULL, "　金属バットは確かに俺の手にあった。",
		   NULL, "Metal sopa kesinlikle elimdeydi.", GetGlobalFlag(GLinemodeSp));
	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode)) { ClearMessage(); } else { OutputLineAll(NULL, "\n", Line_ContinueAfterTyping); }



//　だが…蛇に睨まれた蛙のような俺には何の役にも立たない＠
	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode)) { OutputLineAll("", NULL, Line_ContinueAfterTyping); }
	OutputLine(NULL, "　だが…蛇に睨まれた蛙のような俺には何の役にも立たない。",
		   NULL, "Fakat... yılanın karşısında donakalmış bir kurbağadan farkı olmayan benim için hiçbir işe yaramıyordu.", GetGlobalFlag(GLinemodeSp));
	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode)) { ClearMessage(); } else { OutputLineAll(NULL, "\n", Line_ContinueAfterTyping); }



//　脂汗が全身を伝い、ぽたぽたと零れ落ちるのがわかる…￥
	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode)) { OutputLineAll("", NULL, Line_ContinueAfterTyping); }
	OutputLine(NULL, "　脂汗が全身を伝い、ぽたぽたと零れ落ちるのがわかる…。",
		   NULL, "Soğuk terlerin tüm vücudumdan süzülüp damla damla yere düştüğünü hissedebiliyordum...", Line_Normal);
	ClearMessage();
	DisableWindow();
	ModDrawCharacter(2, 2, "sprite/re1a_warai_a1_", "2", 0, 0, 0, FALSE, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 10, 200, TRUE );

//「…しないよね＠　圭一くんは。＠
	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode)) { OutputLine("<color=#f0953d>レナ</color>", NULL, "<color=#f0953d>Rena</color>", NULL, Line_ContinueAfterTyping); }
	ModPlayVoiceLS(3, 2, "ps3/s01/02/120200256", 256, TRUE);
	OutputLine(NULL, "「…しないよね？",
		   NULL, "\"...Yapmazsın, değil mi?", Line_WaitForInput);
	ModPlayVoiceLS(3, 2, "ps3/s01/02/120200257", 256, TRUE);
	OutputLine(NULL, "　圭一くんは。」",
		   NULL, " Sen yapmazsın, Keiichi-kun.\"", GetGlobalFlag(GLinemodeSp));
	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode)) { ClearMessage(); } else { OutputLineAll(NULL, "\n\n", Line_ContinueAfterTyping); }


//　…無限とも思える時間の檻から、ようやくレナは解放してくれた＠
	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode)) { OutputLineAll("", NULL, Line_ContinueAfterTyping); }
	OutputLine(NULL, "　…無限とも思える時間の檻から、ようやくレナは解放してくれた。",
		   NULL, "...Sonsuzluk kadar uzun gelen o zaman kafesinden Rena nihayet beni azat etti.", GetGlobalFlag(GLinemodeSp));
	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode)) { ClearMessage(); } else { OutputLineAll(NULL, "\n", Line_ContinueAfterTyping); }



//　だがその問いかけは主語を欠いた、とても抽象的なものだった＠
	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode)) { OutputLineAll("", NULL, Line_ContinueAfterTyping); }
	OutputLine(NULL, "　だがその問いかけは主語を欠いた、とても抽象的なものだった。",
		   NULL, "Fakat bu sorusu öznesi olmayan, son derece soyut bir soruydu.", GetGlobalFlag(GLinemodeSp));
	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode)) { ClearMessage(); } else { OutputLineAll(NULL, "\n", Line_ContinueAfterTyping); }



//　ごくりともう一度唾を飲み込み、先を促す￥
	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode)) { OutputLineAll("", NULL, Line_ContinueAfterTyping); }
	OutputLine(NULL, "　ごくりともう一度唾を飲み込み、先を促す。",
		   NULL, "Bir kez daha sertçe yutkunup devam etmesini bekledim.", Line_Normal);
	ClearMessage();

//「……な、…なにをだよ…。＠
	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode)) { OutputLine("<color=#956f6e>圭一</color>", NULL, "<color=#956f6e>Keiichi</color>", NULL, Line_ContinueAfterTyping); }
	ModPlayVoiceLS(3, 1, "ps3/s01/01/120100467", 256, TRUE);
	OutputLine(NULL, "「……な、…なにをだよ…。」",
		   NULL, "\"...N-Neyi...?\"", GetGlobalFlag(GLinemodeSp));
	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode)) { ClearMessage(); } else { OutputLineAll(NULL, "\n", Line_ContinueAfterTyping); }



//　俺が……何をしないって…？＠
	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode)) { OutputLineAll("", NULL, Line_ContinueAfterTyping); }
	OutputLine(NULL, "　俺が……何をしないって…？？",
		   NULL, "Ben... neyi yapmazmışım...??", GetGlobalFlag(GLinemodeSp));
	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode)) { ClearMessage(); } else { OutputLineAll(NULL, "\n", Line_ContinueAfterTyping); }


	DisableWindow();
	ModDrawCharacter(2, 2, "sprite/re1a_hig_muhyou_a1_", "0", 0, 0, 0, FALSE, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 10, 200, TRUE );

//「"転校"」!w3000
	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode)) { OutputLine("<color=#f0953d>レナ</color>", NULL, "<color=#f0953d>Rena</color>", NULL, Line_ContinueAfterTyping); }
	ModPlayVoiceLS(3, 2, "ps3/s01/02/120200258", 256, TRUE);
	OutputLine(NULL, "「“転校”」",
		   NULL, "\"Nakil olmayı.\"", Line_Continue);

	// (backup) SetValidityOfInput( FALSE );
	Wait( 3000 );
	// (backup) SetValidityOfInput( TRUE );

	OutputLineAll(NULL, "", Line_Normal);

	ClearMessage();

	DisableWindow();
	SetValidityOfInput( FALSE );
